{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01NnTYEaxcceipZPWvq4bzwZ","type":"tool_result","content":"// QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version\n// \n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    \n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    // Add seed point\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n    \n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n    \n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\", \n               num_points, threshold);\n        return 1;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"QT Clustering Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of points: %d\\n\", num_points);\n    printf(\"Distance threshold: %.2f\\n\", threshold);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    // Generate synthetic data\n    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);\n    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);\n    \n    // Perform QT clustering\n    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);\n    \n    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(\n        cluster_end - cluster_start);\n    \n    printf(\"Clustering time: %ld ms\\n\", cluster_time.count());\n    printf(\"Clusters found: %zu\\n\", clusters.size());\n    \n    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics\n    int total_clustered = 0;\n    int max_cluster_size = 0;\n    \n    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {\n        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());\n        total_clustered += size;\n        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);\n    }\n    \n    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 : \n        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();\n    \n    printf(\"Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\\n\", \n           total_clustered, num_points, \n           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);\n    printf(\"Average cluster size: %.2f\\n\", avg_cluster_size);\n    printf(\"Maximum cluster size: %d\\n\", max_cluster_size);\n    \n    // Performance metrics\n    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;\n    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;\n    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\\n\", \n           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing\n        std::vector<double> membershipData;\n        membershipData.reserve(num_points);\n        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);\n            }\n        }\n        for (int m : membership) {\n            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));\n        }\n        print_results(membershipData, \"ClusterMembership\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n        const bool Instead of\n// recomputing all member distances for every candidate at every step, the\n// per-candidate maximum distance is maintained incrementally (max is exact\n// regardless of evaluation order), and candidates whose maximum distance has\n// already reached the threshold are dropped since it can only grow.\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                             const std::vector<int>& unclustered_indices,\n                             const Point* __restrict points,\n                             const double threshold,\n                             const int min_required,\n                             Scratch& s) {\n    std::vector<int>& cand = s.candidates;\n    std::vector<double>& md = s.max_dist;\n    std::vector<int>& members = s.members;\n\n    members.clear();\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n\n    // Initial candidate set: all unclustered points except the seed, with their\n    // distance to the seed; prune those already at/over the threshold.\n    cand.clear();\n    const Point ps = points[seed_point];\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n        const int c = unclustered_indices[i];\n        if (c == seed_point) continue;\n        const double d = distance(points[c], ps);\n        if (d < threshold) {\n            md[c] = d;\n            cand.push_back(c);\n        }\n    }\n\n    while (!cand.empty()) {\n        // Upper bound on the final size: everything still eligible gets added.\n        if (static_cast<int>(members.size() + cand.size()) < min_required) {\n            return -1;\n        }\n\n        // Find the candidate with the smallest max distance (first index wins ties)\n        int closest = -1;\n        double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cand.size(); ++i) {\n            const int c = cand[i];\n            const double d = md[c];\n            if (d < min_diameter) {\n                min_diameter = d;\n                closest = c;\n            }\n        }\n        if (closest < 0) break;\n\n        members.push_back(closest);\n\n        // Update max distances w.r.t. the newly added member and compact the\n        // candidate list, removing the chosen point and those now >= threshold.\n        const Point pn = points[closest];\n        size_t w = 0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cand.size(); ++i) {\n            const int c = cand[i];\n            if (c == closest) continue;\n            const double d = distance(points[c], pn);\n            const double m = (d > md[c]) ? d : md[c];\n            if (m < threshold) {\n                md[c] = m;\n                cand[w++] = c;\n            }\n        }\n        cand.resize(w);\n    }\n\n    const int card = static_cast<int>(members.size());\n    return (card < min_required) ? -1 : card;\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    // Initialize unclustered indices (kept in ascending order throughout)\n    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n\n    const Point* pts = points.data();\n\n    // Shared state for the current outer iteration\n    int max_cardinality = -1;\n    int best_seed = -1;\n    std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n    int global_best = 0;   // best cardinality seen so far (used only for pruning)\n    bool done = false;\n\n    // One parallel region for the whole algorithm: threads keep their scratch\n    // buffers across outer iterations and only synchronize at the barriers.\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        Scratch s;\n        s.max_dist.resize(N);\n        s.candidates.reserve(N);\n        s.members.reserve(N);\n        std::vector<int> local_members;\n        local_members.reserve(N);\n\n        // Main clustering loop (all threads execute it in lock-step)\n        while (true) {\n            #pragma omp barrier\n            if (done) break;\n\n            const int num_seeds = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n\n            int local_max = -1;\n            int local_seed = -1;\n\n            // Try each unclustered point as a seed, in parallel.  Each thread keeps\n            // its own best; the global best is the largest cluster, with ties broken\n            // by the lowest seed index (equivalent to the sequential first-wins rule\n            // since seeds are visited in ascending order there).\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 2) nowait\n            for (int i = 0; i < num_seeds; ++i) {\n                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n\n                // A seed can only displace the best if it reaches at least the\n                // best cardinality (a tie may win on the lower index).  Reading a\n                // stale/lower value only weakens the prune, never the result.\n                int required;\n                #pragma omp atomic read\n                required = global_best;\n\n                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, unclustered_indices,\n                                                                 pts, threshold, required, s);\n                if (cardinality < 0) continue;\n\n                if (cardinality > local_max ||\n                    (cardinality == local_max && seed < local_seed)) {\n                    local_max = cardinality;\n                    local_seed = seed;\n                    local_members = s.members;\n                    if (cardinality > required) {\n                        #pragma omp critical(best_update)\n                        {\n                            if (cardinality > global_best) global_best = cardinality;\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            #pragma omp critical(best_update)\n            {\n                if (local_max > max_cardinality ||\n                    (local_max == max_cardinality && local_seed >= 0 && local_seed < best_seed)) {\n                    max_cardinality = local_max;\n                    best_seed = local_seed;\n                    best_cluster_members.swap(local_members);\n                }\n            }\n\n            #pragma omp barrier\n            #pragma omp single\n            {\n                // If we found a cluster, add it\n                if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n                    Cluster cluster;\n                    cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n                    cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n                    clusters.push_back(cluster);\n\n                    // Mark all members as clustered\n                    for (size_t i = 0; The candidate clusters of one iteration are\n    // evaluated in parallel across seeds.  Only one barrier per iteration is\n    // needed: every thread publishes its local best in a per-thread slot, and\n    // after the barrier all threads redundantly determine the winner and update\n    // a private copy of the bookkeeping (cheap, O(N)), avoiding critical\n    // sections and single regions.  The winner's member list is double-buffered\n    // so that the owning thread can start the next iteration while others may\n    // still be reading the previous winner.\n    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    std::vector<ThreadResult> results(max_threads);\n    int global_best = 0;   // best cardinality seen in this iteration (pruning only)\n    int global_best_next = 0;\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();\n        const int nthreads = omp_get_num_threads();\n\n        Scratch s;\n        s.max_dist.resize(N);\n        s.candidates.reserve(N);\n        s.members.reserve(N);\n        std::vector<int> local_members[2];\n        local_members[0].reserve(N);\n        local_members[1].reserve(N);\n\n        // Private bookkeeping (identical in every thread)\n        std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n        std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n        unclustered_indices.reserve(N);\n        for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n\n        for (int iter = 0; !unclustered_indices.empty(); ++iter) {\n            const int num_seeds = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n            std::vector<int>& my_members = local_members[iter & 1];\n\n            int local_max = -1;\n            int local_seed = -1;\n\n            // Try each unclustered point as a seed, in parallel.\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 2) nowait\n            for (int i = 0; i < num_seeds; ++i) {\n                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n\n                // A seed can only displace the best if it reaches at least the\n                // best cardinality (a tie may win on the lower index).  Reading a\n                // stale/lower value only weakens the prune, never the result.\n                int required;\n                #pragma omp atomic read\n                required = global_best;\n\n                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, unclustered_indices,\n                                                                 pts, threshold, required, s);\n                if (cardinality < 0) continue;\n\n                if (cardinality > local_max ||\n                    (cardinality == local_max && seed < local_seed)) {\n                    local_max = cardinality;\n                    local_seed = seed;\n                    my_members = s.members;\n                    if (cardinality > required) {\n                        // Monotonic max update; The candidate clusters of one iteration are\n    // evaluated in parallel across seeds.\n    //\n    // Only one barrier per iteration is needed: every thread publishes its\n    // local best in a per-thread slot; after the barrier all threads\n    // redundantly determine the winner and update a private copy of the\n    // bookkeeping (cheap, O(N)).  No critical sections or single regions.\n    //\n    // All cross-iteration shared state (result slots, member buffers, pruning\n    // bound) is double-buffered by iteration parity: a buffer used in\n    // iteration k is next written in iteration k+2, which no thread can reach\n    // before every thread has passed barrier k+1, i.e. finished reading it.\n    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    std::vector<ThreadResult> results[2] = {\n        std::vector<ThreadResult>(max_threads), std::vector<ThreadResult>(max_threads)};\n    PaddedInt global_best[2]; i < N; ++i) unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n\n        for (int iter = 0; !unclustered_indices.empty(); ++iter) {\n            const int parity = iter & 1;\n            const int num_seeds = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n            std::vector<int>& my_members = local_members[parity];\n            int& bound = global_best[parity].value;\n\n            int local_max = -1;\n            int local_seed = -1;\n\n            // Try each unclustered point as a seed, in parallel.\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 2) nowait\n            for (int i = 0; i < num_seeds; ++i) {\n                const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n\n                // A seed can only displace the best if it reaches at least the\n                // best cardinality (a tie may win on the lower index).  Reading a\n                // stale/lower value only weakens the prune, never the result.\n                int required;\n                #pragma omp atomic read\n                required = bound;\n\n                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, unclustered_indices,\n                                                                 pts, threshold, required, s);\n                if (cardinality < 0) continue;\n\n                if (cardinality > local_max ||\n                    (cardinality == local_max && seed < local_seed)) {\n                    local_max = cardinality;\n                    local_seed = seed;\n                    my_members = s.members;\n                    if (cardinality > required) {\n                        // Monotonic max update (atomic compare-and-swap loop).\n                        int cur = required;\n                        while (cardinality > cur) {\n                            int observed;\n                            #pragma omp atomic capture\n                            { observed = bound; bound = (bound > cardinality) ? bound : cardinality; }\n                            if (observed >= cardinality) break;\n                            cur = observed;\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            ThreadResult& mine = results[parity][tid];\n            mine.cardinality = local_max;\n            mine.seed = local_seed;\n            mine.members = &my_members;\n\n            #pragma omp barrier\n\n            // Every thread determines the winner of this iteration\n            int max_cardinality = -1;\n            int best_seed = -1;\n            const std::vector<int>* best_cluster_members = nullptr;\n            const ThreadResult* res = results[parity].data();\n            for (int t = 0; t < nthreads; ++t) {\n                const ThreadResult& r = res[t];\n                if (r.cardinality > max_cardinality ||\n                    (r.cardinality == max_cardinality && r.seed >= 0 && r.seed < best_seed)) {\n                    max_cardinality = r.cardinality;\n                    best_seed = r.seed;\n                    best_cluster_members = r.members;\n                }\n            }\n\n            if (!(best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0)) {\n                break; // No more clusters can be formed (same decision in all threads)\n            }\n\n            if (tid == 0) {\n                // Record the cluster\n                Cluster cluster;\n                cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n                cluster.members = *best_cluster_members;\n                clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n\n                // Reset this parity's pruning bound for iteration iter+2.  All\n                // reads of it (iteration iter) completed before the barrier\n                // above, and iteration iter+2 cannot start anywhere before this\n                // thread reaches barrier iter+1.\n                #pragma omp atomic write\n                bound = 0;\n            }\n\n            // Update private bookkeeping: mark members as clustered and remove\n            // them from the (ascending) unclustered list.\n            for (size_t i = 0; The clustering loop needs one barrier per\n// cluster found; with many threads (e.g. 256 across two sockets) the runtime\n// barrier may put waiting threads to sleep and the wake-up latency then\n// dominates the (short) per-iteration work.  Spinning keeps that latency in\n// the microsecond range independent of runtime tuning.\nclass SpinBarrier {\n  public:\n    explicit SpinBarrier(int n) : count_(n), nthreads_(n), sense_(0) {}\n\n    void wait(int& local_sense) {\n        local_sense ^= 1;\n        if (count_.fetch_sub(1, std::memory_order_acq_rel) == 1) {\n            count_.store(nthreads_, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n            sense_.store(local_sense, std::memory_order_release);\n        } else {\n            int spins = 0;\n            while (sense_.load(std::memory_order_acquire) != local_sense) {\n                if (++spins > 4096) {\n                    std::this_thread::yield();\n                    spins = 0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n  private:\n    alignas(64) std::atomic<int> count_;\n    const int nthreads_;\n    alignas(64) std::atomic<int> sense_;\n};\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    const Point* pts = points.data();\n\n    // Each outer iteration selects the largest candidate cluster (ties broken\n    // by lowest seed index, matching the sequential first-wins order), removes\n    // its members, and repeats.  The candidate clusters of one iteration are\n    // evaluated in parallel across seeds.\n    //\n    // Only one barrier per iteration is needed: every thread publishes its\n    // local best in a per-thread slot; after the barrier all threads\n    // redundantly determine the winner and update a private copy of the\n    // bookkeeping (cheap, O(N)).  No critical sections or single regions.\n    //\n    // All cross-iteration shared state (result slots, member buffers, pruning\n    // bound, size estimates) is double-buffered by iteration parity: a buffer\n    // used in iteration k is next written in iteration k+2, which no thread\n    // can reach before every thread has passed barrier k+1, i.e. finished\n    // reading it.\n    //\n    // Seeds are processed in order of decreasing estimated cluster size (the\n    // size, or the pruning bound, obtained in the previous iteration; the\n    // neighbour count within the threshold for the first iteration).  This\n    // makes the best cardinality known early so most seeds are pruned, and\n    // starts the expensive seeds first for better load balance.  Ordering does\n    // not affect the result: the winner is chosen by (size, lowest index).\n    const int max_threads = omp_get_max_threads();\n    std::vector<ThreadResult> results[2] = {\n        std::vector<ThreadResult>(max_threads), std::vector<ThreadResult>(max_threads)};\n    // Member buffers live outside the parallel region so that a winner's list\n    // stays valid while slower threads still copy it in the final iteration.\n    std::vector<std::vector<int>> member_bufs(2 * static_cast<size_t>(max_threads));\n    PaddedAtomic global_best[2];   // best cardinality seen this iteration (pruning only)\n    std::vector<int> estimate[2] = {std::vector<int>(N, 0), std::vector<int>(N, 0)};\n\n    #pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const int tid = omp_get_thread_num();\n        const int nthreads = omp_get_num_threads();\n\n        static SpinBarrier* barrier_ptr = nullptr;\n        #pragma omp single\n        barrier_ptr = new SpinBarrier(nthreads);\n        SpinBarrier& barrier = *barrier_ptr;\n        int sense = 0;\n\n        Scratch s;\n        s.max_dist.resize(N);\n        s.candidates.reserve(N);\n        s.members.reserve(N);\n        std::vector<int>* const local_members = &member_bufs[2 * static_cast<size_t>(tid)];\n\n        // Private bookkeeping (identical in every thread)\n        std::vector<char> clustered(N, 0);\n        std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n        unclustered_indices.reserve(N);\n        for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n        std::vector<int> order;\n        order.reserve(N);\n\n        // Initial estimate: 1 + number of points within the threshold\n        {\n            int* est0 = estimate[0].data();\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 16)\n            for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n                const Point pi = pts[i];\n                int cnt = 0;\n                for (int j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n                    cnt += (distance(pts[j], pi) < threshold) ? 1 : 0;\n                }\n                est0[i] = cnt;   // includes i itself\n            }\n            // implicit barrier\n        }\n\n        for (int iter = 0; !unclustered_indices.empty(); ++iter) {\n            const int parity = iter & 1;\n            const int num_seeds = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n            std::vector<int>& my_members = local_members[parity];\n            std::atomic<int>& bound = global_best[parity].value;\n            const int* est = estimate[parity].data();\n            int* est_next = estimate[parity ^ 1].data();\n\n            // Processing order: decreasing estimate, then increasing index\n            order.assign(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end());\n            std::stable_sort(order.begin(), order.end(),\n                             [est](int a, int b) { return est[a] > est[b]; });\n\n            int local_max = -1;\n            int local_seed = -1;\n\n            // Try each unclustered point as a seed, in parallel.\n            #pragma omp for schedule(dynamic, 1) nowait\n            for (int i = 0; i < num_seeds; ++i) {\n                const int seed = order[i];\n\n                // A seed can only displace the best if it reaches at least the\n                // best cardinality (a tie may win on the lower index).  Reading a\n                // stale/lower value only weakens the prune, never the result.\n                int required = bound.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);\n\n                int upper = 0;\n                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, unclustered_indices,\n                                                                 pts, threshold, required, s,\n                                                                 upper);\n                est_next[seed] = (cardinality < 0) ? upper : cardinality;\n                if (cardinality < 0) continue;\n\n                if (cardinality > local_max ||\n                    (cardinality == local_max && seed < local_seed)) {\n                    local_max = cardinality;\n                    local_seed = seed;\n                    my_members = s.members;\n                    if (cardinality > required) {\n                        // Monotonic max update (compare-and-swap loop).\n                        int cur = required;\n                        while (cardinality > cur &&\n                               !bound.compare_exchange_weak(cur, cardinality,\n                                                            std::memory_order_relaxed)) {\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            ThreadResult& mine = results[parity][tid];\n            mine.cardinality = local_max;\n            mine.seed = local_seed;\n            mine.members = &my_members;\n\n            barrier.wait(sense);\n\n            // Every thread determines the winner of this iteration\n            int max_cardinality = -1;\n            int best_seed = -1;\n            const std::vector<int>* best_cluster_members = nullptr;\n            const ThreadResult* res = results[parity].data();\n            for (int t = 0; t < nthreads; ++t) {\n                const ThreadResult& r = res[t];\n                if (r.cardinality > max_cardinality ||\n                    (r.cardinality == max_cardinality && r.seed >= 0 && r.seed < best_seed)) {\n                    max_cardinality = r.cardinality;\n                    best_seed = r.seed;\n                    best_cluster_members = r.members;\n                }\n            }\n\n            if (!(best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0)) {\n                break; All\n                // reads of it (iteration iter) completed before the barrier\n                // above, and iteration iter+2 cannot start anywhere before this\n                // thread reaches barrier iter+1.\n                bound.store(0, std::memory_order_relaxed);\n            }\n\n            // Update private bookkeeping: mark members as clustered and remove\n            // them from the (ascending) unclustered list.\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members->size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[(*best_cluster_members)[i]] = 1;\n            }\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx] != 0; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        }\n\n        #pragma omp barrier\n        #pragma omp single\n        {\n            delete barrier_ptr;\n            barrier_ptr = nullptr;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n'''\nsrc = src[:start] + new_core + src[end:]\n# generateCandidateCluster: add 'upper' out-param\nsrc = src.replace('''                             const int min_required,\n                             Scratch& s) {''','''                             const int min_required,\n                             Scratch& s,\n                             int& upper_bound) {''')\nsrc = src.replace('''        if (static_cast<int>(members.size() + cand.size()) < min_required) {\n            return -1;\n        }''','''        upper_bound = static_cast<int>(members.size() + cand.size());\n        if (upper_bound < min_required) {\n            return -1;\n        }''')\nsrc = src.replace('''    const int card = static_cast<int>(members.size());\n    return (card < min_required) ? Reading a\n                // stale/lower value only weakens the prune, never the result.\n                int required = bound.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);\n\n                int upper = 0;\n                const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, unclustered_indices,\n                                                                 pts, threshold, required, s,\n                                                                 upper);\n                est_next[seed] = (cardinality < 0) ? upper : cardinality;\n                if (cardinality < 0) continue;\n\n                if (cardinality > local_max ||\n                    (cardinality == local_max && seed < local_seed)) {\n                    local_max = cardinality;\n                    local_seed = seed;\n                    my_members = s.members;\n                    if (cardinality > required) {\n                        // Monotonic max update (compare-and-swap loop).\n                        int cur = required;\n                        while (cardinality > cur &&\n                               !bound.compare_exchange_weak(cur, cardinality,\n                                                            std::memory_order_relaxed)) {\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            ThreadResult& mine = results[parity][tid];\n            mine.cardinality = local_max;\n            mine.seed = local_seed;\n            mine.members = &my_members;\n\n            barrier.wait(sense, nthreads);\n\n            // Every thread determines the winner of this iteration\n            int max_cardinality = -1;\n            int best_seed = -1;\n            const std::vector<int>* best_cluster_members = nullptr;\n            const ThreadResult* res = results[parity].data();\n            for (int t = 0; The clustering is parallelized with OpenMP in the two existing files only, builds cleanly with no warnings, and produces bit-identical result hashes to the original for every configuration tested at 1, 3, 64, 128 and 256 threads.\n\n**What changed in `qtclustering.cpp`**\n\n- **Seeds evaluated in parallel.** One parallel region covers the whole run. Each iteration, threads grow candidate clusters for different seeds, publish their local best to a per-thread slot, and after a single barrier every thread picks the global winner. Ties go to the lowest seed index, which is exactly the original's first-wins order.\n- **Incremental candidate growth.** Each candidate's maximum distance to the cluster is maintained as members are added, and candidates that reach the threshold are dropped. Adding a member now costs O(N) instead of O(N·k), which alone made the single-threaded run 20 to 50 times faster.\n- **Safe pruning.** A seed whose remaining candidate pool cannot reach the best cardinality found so far in the iteration is abandoned. It can never be chosen, so the result is unchanged. Seeds are processed in order of estimated size so the bound rises early.\n- **No synchronization overhead in the loop.** No critical sections or single regions. Cross-iteration state is double-buffered by parity, and a self-initializing atomic spin barrier replaces the runtime barrier, which was sleeping and waking 256 threads slowly. All per-thread scratch is preallocated by the main thread, so no heap allocation happens inside the region.\n- **Validation diameter check** uses a parallel max reduction. The data generator and output are untouched.\n\n**`CMakeLists.txt`** adds `find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)` and links `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`.\n\nClustering time in milliseconds on this 2-socket, 128-core EPYC host:\n\n| Points, threshold | Original | 1 thread | 64 | 128 | 256 |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 1000, 2.0 | 7020 | 119 | 9 | 11 | 43 |\n| 2000, 3.0 | 183510 | 643 | 19 | 17 | 42 |\n| 3000, 1.0 | 156224 | 2309 | 80 | 68 | 124 |\n| 5000, 2.0 | not run | 4482 | 103 | 71 | 89 |\n\n**Two things to know**\n\n- The 256-thread default is slower than 128 only because unbound threads get preempted on this shared, loaded host.
